2026-09-13  R-430  Began the differentiating spine (founder-approved pivot after the R-429 strategy discussion): the Ecosystem Scope Compiler. Per the Master Architecture Spec (section 2), competitors turn "create a food delivery app" into a single customer screen; OmniStackAI now proposes the whole business ecosystem BEFORE generation. New Python-3.13-stdlib-only `services/agent-engine/src/omnistackai_agent_engine/intake/scope_compiler.py` turns a plain-English business prompt into a framework-neutral `ScopeProposal`: the detected domain (with a confidence score and the matched keywords), the business actors, the multi-surface app ecosystem (a customer app plus merchant/driver/provider portals and an admin dashboard, as the domain warrants), and 1-3 build-scope options — Complete Business Platform (recommended), Customer Experience Only, and Custom/Multi-Surface — plus at most 3 high-materiality clarifying questions. Classification is a deterministic curated keyword heuristic over a 10-domain `DOMAIN_LIBRARY` (food-delivery, rideshare, marketplace, e-commerce, b2b-saas, healthcare-clinic, booking, learning, social, blog-cms): `classify_domain(prompt)` scores weighted keywords (a multi-word phrase weighs more than a single token), the highest-scoring domain at/above a threshold wins with a stable tie-break by library order, and it returns `None` (→ a single-app `custom-application` fallback) when nothing matches. `propose_ecosystem(prompt)` assembles the actors, surfaces, options (Customer Experience Only carries only the customer-audience surfaces as a strict subset), and questions. It has no model, network, clock, or randomness, so it is pure — the same prompt yields a byte-identical proposal — and runs under `task verify`; any cheap-LLM refinement of the classification is a later, opt-in brick (mirroring `nl_to_ir.py` core vs `live_run.py`). All value objects are frozen dataclasses (`Actor`, `AppSurface`, `ScopeOption`, `ScopeProposal`, `DomainMatch`, `DomainSpec`) with JSON-safe `to_dict()`, exported from `intake/__init__.py`. Added a deterministic CLI `intake/scope_propose.py` wired as `task agent-engine:scope:propose -- "<prompt>"` (+ `scripts/agent-engine.sh scope-propose`, usage string updated) so the founder can see it: `"Create a food delivery app where customers order from restaurants and couriers deliver"` → domain food-delivery (confidence 1.0) → Customer Ordering App + Merchant Portal + Courier Dispatch App + Super-Admin Dashboard, with Complete/Customer-only/Custom options and 2 materiality questions. This is the first brick of the spine and is NOT yet wired into IR/repo generation — mapping each proposed `AppSurface` to an Application IR so "Complete Business Platform" materializes multiple owned repos from one prompt is the next brick. No IR/adapter/DB-engine change (PostgreSQL stays); `task verify` remains offline/deterministic.

2026-09-13  R-429  Made a generated Next.js app pass strict `tsc --noEmit` with ZERO errors, so generated apps are no longer blocked from a production `next build`. Ran the R-428 opt-in gate to get the real error list: a generated `minimal-blog` reported 84 errors — 4 in `node_modules/next` (a missing `skipLibCheck`) plus 80 in our generated code across 19 files, in 8 mechanical classes, all fixed at `codegen/nextjs.py` (and the generated `tsconfig.json`): (G0) the generated tsconfig now sets `"skipLibCheck": true` — Next.js's own default — so dependency `.d.ts` files are not type-checked; (G1) `components/context-menu.tsx` no longer double-exports — its trailing `export { … }` block re-listed 13 names that are already `export const` (TS2323 "cannot redeclare" / TS2484 "conflicts"), now reduced to `export { ContextMenu }` for the compound only; (G2) internal arrow/function sub-component aliases in `audio-player`/`geo-map`/`pdf-viewer` are typed `typeof XInner & { displayName?: string }` so assigning `.displayName` is allowed (no runtime change); (G3) the compound components `color-picker` and `pin-input` are rebuilt as a typed compound — the `forwardRef` result is a `…Base` const cast to `type XComponent = typeof XBase & { Sub: … }`, so both the `.Sub = …` attachments and consumer `<X.Sub/>` usage are on the component type (named `export { X }` + `export default` preserved); (G4) `BannerProps`/`CarouselProps`/`CheckboxProps`/`CodeBlockProps`/`CodeBlockCopyButtonProps` now `Omit` the conflicting inherited DOM attribute they redefine (`title`/`onSelect`/`defaultChecked`/`onCopy`) so the interface no longer "incorrectly extends" `HTMLAttributes`/`ButtonHTMLAttributes` (TS2430); (G5) DOM/SVG element ref type annotations changed from `React.RefObject<T | null>` to `React.RefObject<T>` so they are assignable to a JSX `ref=` (TS2322) — mutable `React.MutableRefObject<T | null>` callback-ref casts and value refs (`AbortController`/`number`) were left untouched; (G6) `components/terminal.tsx` destructuring defaults `variant = "default"` became `variant = "minimal"` (a valid `TerminalVariant`, whose union is `terminal|neon|glass|minimal`; there is no `=== "minimal"` branch, so the rendered output is byte-identical), scoped strictly to the terminal template so the other 65 `variant = "default"` defaults — where "default" IS a valid variant — are unchanged; (G7) added `isUnchanged?: boolean` to the `SplitDiffRow` type; (G8) the exported `api` object now includes the `…WithCount` LIST/LIST_BY client methods (they were emitted as standalone functions but omitted from the object, so hooks calling `api.listPostsWithCount(…)` failed with TS2339/2551), and the generated LIST/LIST_BY hooks now always forward a fresh `requestParams` object literal (an object-literal spread has an implicit index signature and so is assignable to the client's flat `Record<string, …>` param type, whereas a bare `UseListParams` interface value is not) with `...options` spread first, so a caller's `options` can no longer override the hook's computed params or its abort signal (now consistent with the existing GET/CREATE hooks). Extended the opt-in gate (`scripts/agent-engine.sh web-typecheck`) to treat a non-zero `tsc` exit as failure and print PASS/FAIL: `task agent-engine:web-typecheck -- minimal-blog` and `-- rideshare-favourites` both report PASSED (0 errors) after a fresh generate + `pnpm install --ignore-scripts` + `tsc --noEmit`. Added deterministic regression guards to `services/agent-engine/tests/test_generated_tsx_compile.py` (generated `tsconfig` sets `skipLibCheck`; no name is both `export const/function` and re-listed in an `export { }` block; no `React.RefObject<HTMLxxx | null>` annotations; every `api.<method>` a generated hook calls exists on the exported `api` object) and updated 7 hook/component test files' exact assertions to the corrected emitted strings. `task verify`'s 2,877 tests pass (offline; +4 new asserts); lint/security/env and both builder demos (152 / 149 files) pass; snapshot diff-invariance unaffected; 0 local/cloud model calls. This removes the last blocker to a production `next build` for generated apps; `task verify` stays offline/deterministic and the typecheck gate remains opt-in/live. NOTE: a running `studio:preview` holds the old generator in memory — restart it so a fresh build uses the fixed generator.

2026-09-13  R-427  Fixed a real generated-code bug that made generated Next.js web apps fail to compile (HTTP 500) — found by actually running a generated app through the Studio live preview: the backend was healthy (`/posts` 200) but the web app returned 500 with an SWC syntax error (`Expected '</', got ':'`) on a single-brace JSX inline style in the generated form/list/detail screens. Root cause: 14 f-string templates in `codegen/nextjs.py` (screen-header role badges, `<h1>`/`<h2>` titles, and detail `<dt>`/`<dd>` definition lists) wrote `style={{ ... }}`, which Python collapses to single-brace `style={ ... }` in the emitted TSX — invalid JSX, which requires the double-brace `style={{ ... }}` (a JSX expression container wrapping an object literal). The fix rewrites those f-string styles to quadruple braces `style={{{{ ... }}}}` so they emit valid `style={{ ... }}`; only f-string lines were changed (regular and raw-string `r"""..."""` component templates, which were already correct, were left untouched), and no component template, screen layout/structure, styling value, or generated logic changed. Added a deterministic regression test (`test_generated_screen_styles.py`) that generates both example IRs and asserts no generated `.tsx` contains a single-brace object-literal inline style (`style={ key: ...`) — precise enough to allow valid single-brace expression styles (`style={expr}`); it is RED before the fix (catching `app/favourites/page.tsx` and the blog screens) and GREEN after, so this class of bug cannot recur. `task verify` had not caught it because it asserts generated code as strings and never compiles the TSX. 3 focused tests plus `task verify`'s 2,870 tests pass; lint/security/env and both builder demos (152 / 149 files) pass; snapshot diff-invariance is unaffected. Verification used deterministic string generation only; 0 local/cloud model calls. NOTE: a running `studio:preview` holds the old generator in memory — restart it so a fresh build compiles and the embedded preview renders.

Every generator-owned table, column, relation-column, join-table, and index identifier is now defensively double-quoted consistently in DDL, fixture INSERTs, Python repositories, and Go stores, while logical snake_case code/API names remain unchanged. Added stable topological ordering for entity tables and fixture groups over many-to-one/one-to-one dependencies: parents precede dependants, independent entities retain source order, self-references remain valid, many-to-many tables remain after entities, and non-self cycles raise a deterministic explanatory `ValueError`. Added 7 focused SQL-safety tests and updated only exact SQL assertions affected by intentional quoting. Verification: 186 related regressions pass; `task verify` 2,808 tests; lint/security/env and minimal-blog (152 files) plus rideshare-favourites (149 files) demos pass; generated Python parsed and representative Go stores passed `gofmt`. Live rollback-only PostgreSQL proof created a reserved-name User/Order schema, both tables, and index successfully, then rolled back. Standard-library-only platform code, no dependency/IR/DB-engine/infrastructure change, 0 local/cloud model calls.

2026-09-13  R-419  Added the turnkey local run (`services/agent-engine/src/omnistackai_agent_engine/localrun/`) — BRICK 4 of the "chat -> create an app" front door, closing the local "chat -> create -> RUN" loop: a generated app repo now boots in one command. New `localrun` package (Python 3.13 stdlib only), following the platform "deterministic plan + opt-in executor" pattern. `plan.py` provides `RunStep`/`RunPlan` dataclasses and `build_run_plan(repo_dir, *, db_container, db_user, db_password, db_host, db_port, maintenance_db, db_name=None, api_port=8000, web_port=3000, jwt_secret=...)` which inspects a generated repo (backend flavor python via `services/api/requirements.txt` / go via `go.mod`|`main.go`; migrations under `services/api/migrations/*.sql`; web via `apps/web/package.json`) and composes an ordered, JSON-safe plan: DROP+CREATE a clean per-app PostgreSQL database in the local container, apply each migration (piped on stdin, sorted), start the backend (`uvicorn app.main:app` with `DATABASE_URL`/`JWT_SECRET`, or `go run .`), and start the web app with `./node_modules/.bin/next dev` (the Next binary directly, NEVER `pnpm dev`) + `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL`; `RunPlan.to_dict()` is JSON-safe and masks the DB password. `run.py` is the opt-in executor (`task agent-engine:app:run -- <dir>`, Task `deps: [db:up]`, excluded from static verify): it runs the setup steps synchronously (skipping an existing `.venv`/`node_modules`), launches the two servers, polls the backend `/healthz`, prints the URLs, and terminates both cleanly on Ctrl+C. Verified working END-TO-END on the founder's Mac: `task agent-engine:app:run -- ~/omnistackai-blog-run` -> Postgres up, DB recreated, both migrations applied, backend venv+install, uvicorn on :8000 (`/healthz` 200, `/posts` returned the 2 seeded posts), web install (`pnpm install --ignore-scripts`), Next.js on :3000 (HTTP 200). Fixed a real bug found in testing: a bare `pnpm install` exits 1 on pnpm 11's `ERR_PNPM_IGNORED_BUILDS` (sharp) -> the plan uses `pnpm install --ignore-scripts` (sharp's native build is not needed for `next dev`). Running real migrations also SURFACED two pre-existing schema-generator defects that `task verify` cannot catch (it never executes SQL): (1) reserved-word identifiers are not quoted (a "Order" entity emits `CREATE TABLE order (` -> Postgres syntax error); (2) `CREATE TABLE` order is entity order, not FK-dependency order (an `ingredient`->`recipe` FK emits `ingredient` first -> "relation recipe does not exist") — to be fixed in R-420. Python 3.13 stdlib-only, 0 new dependencies, offline/deterministic verify (12 new tests in test_localrun_plan.py; 2,801 total; lint/security/env and builder:demo pass; 0 model calls in verify).

## [R-334] Generated Accessible Reusable Stepper / Multi-step Wizard Component – 2026-09-11

### Added
- `components/stepper.tsx` emitted in every generated Next.js web application
  - **Types**: `StepperOrientation`, `StepStatus`, `StepperVariant`, `StepDef`, `StepperProps`, `StepContentProps`
  - **Stepper root**: horizontal and vertical orientation via CSS flex; controlled (`value`/`onChange`) and uncontrolled (`defaultValue`) modes
  - **StepIndicator**: numeric (idle), checkmark SVG (completed), X SVG (error), animated focus ring (active); dots variant
  - **StepConnector**: solid line that fills (primary colour) for completed steps; adapts to orientation
  - **StepContent**: `role="tabpanel"`, `aria-labelledby`, `hidden` attribute, renders children only when active
  - **useStepperState hook**: `goNext`, `goPrev`, `goTo`, `canGoNext`, `canGoPrev`, `currentStep`
  - **Progress variant**: `role="progressbar"` bar above the track showing % completion
  - **WAI-ARIA**: `role="tablist"`, `aria-orientation`, `role="tab"`, `aria-selected`, `aria-controls`, `role="tabpanel"`, `aria-labelledby`, `aria-disabled`
  - **Keyboard**: `ArrowRight`/`ArrowLeft` (horizontal), `ArrowDown`/`ArrowUp` (vertical), `Home`, `End`
  - **Zero external dependencies**; 100% diff-invariant across `ir.description` changes
- `render_stepper_component()` exported from `omnistackai_agent_engine.codegen`
- 19 new unit tests in `test_stepper_component.py`; total test count: 1,329

## [R-335] Generated Accessible Reusable File Upload / Dropzone Component – 2026-09-11

### Added
- `components/file-upload.tsx` emitted in every generated Next.js web application
  - **Types**: `FileUploadStatus`, `FileUploadVariant`, `FileEntry`, `FileUploadProps`
  - **Drag-and-drop zone**: `onDragOver`/`onDragLeave`/`onDrop` with animated dashed-border drag state
  - **Click-to-browse**: hidden `<input type="file">` triggered by zone click / Enter / Space
  - **Validation**: `accept` MIME/extension check, `maxSize` byte limit, `maxFiles` cap — all with inline error messages
  - **FileRow list**: filename + formatted size (B/KB/MB), image thumbnail preview, upload progressbar (`role="progressbar"`), accessible remove button (`aria-label`)
  - **Avatar variant**: 96 × 96 circular crop with `CameraIcon`, hover overlay, drag target
  - **Compact variant**: reduced padding, no upload icon
  - **WAI-ARIA**: `role="button"`, `aria-label`, `aria-describedby`, `aria-disabled`, `aria-live="polite"` status region
  - **Keyboard**: `Enter` / `Space` opens file picker
  - **Zero external dependencies**; 100% diff-invariant across `ir.description` changes
- `render_file_upload_component()` exported from `omnistackai_agent_engine.codegen`
- 20 new unit tests in `test_file_upload_component.py`; total test count: 1,349
